D0: F[off] += new(1-new) - old(1-old).
For q in positions 0..n-1, q≠p:

   old_plus = old*(1-h_q)  (h_p was old, h_q unchanged if single) ; new_plus = new*(1-h_q); F[off+d] += new_plus - old_plus.
   old_minus = h_q*(1-old); new_minus = h_q*(1-new); F[off-d] += new_minus - old_minus.

   old_plus = h_q*(1-old); new_plus = h_q*(1-new); F[off+d] += new_plus - old_plus.
   old_minus = old*(1-h_q); new_minus = new*(1-h_q); F[off-d] += new_minus - old_minus.
This can be vectorized using arrays. For a single h change, we can compute all q contributions without loop:

For q<p:
 d = p - idx (1..p)
 F[off + d] += hq_left*(new - old) ? Since new_plus - old_plus = (new-old)*h_q.

For q>p:

Function `evaluate_f(h)` returns maxF*dx. Need compute F with `compute_F(h)`.

`compute_F(h)`:
```
n=len(h); L=2*n-1; off=n-1; F=np.zeros(L)
F[off]=np.sum(h*(1-h))
for d in range(1,n):
    a=h[:-d]; b=h[d:]
    F[off+d]=np.dot(a,1-b)
    F[off-d]=np.dot(b,1-a)
return F
```
This matches objective? Example pair contributions. Max F * dx. Need verify for k=0 all h_i(1-h_i). For full correlate max includes d=0 term? yes. For binary, F[off]=0. For n=2 h=[1,0], target1. d=1: F[off+1]=h0(1-h1)=1, F[off-1]=h1(1-h0)=0. Max=1 dx=1 -> C=1. Seq [0,1]: max=1. C5 min? n=2 maybe 1.

`update_F_single(h, F, p, old, new)` modifies F. Need h[p] not used? It uses h[:p] and h[p+1:] current excluding p. We can call with h currently old at p or not? It only uses other coordinates, so h[p] irrelevant. But to be safe, set h[p]=old before? For applying p then q, after setting h[p]=new, update_F uses h[p+1:] includes q old (if q>p) or h[:p] includes q old. Good. Then set h[q]=newq and update. For revert, need update with old/new swapped and current h other coordinates final.

Precompute offsets for each n? Since n may vary. Create in globals: `_OFFSETS={n: (left_offsets, right_offsets)}`. But top-level functions can compute on fly using np.arange; cache dict. For n up to 512, storing arrays total O(n^2) maybe 262k ints, okay. But if many n, memory okay. For speed, precompute for each n used.
`left_offsets[p] = p - np.arange(p)` (dtype int) length p. `right_offsets[p] = np.arange(1, n-p)` length n-p-1.

Update:
```
def add_single(h, F, p, old, new):
    delta = new - old
    if delta == 0: return
    dx0 = new*(1-new) - old*(1-old)
    off = _OFFSET[n]
    F[off] += dx0
    if p>0:
       dl = lefts[p]
       arr = h[:p]
       F[off+dl] += delta * arr
       F[off-dl] -= delta * arr
    if p<n-1:
       dr = rights[p]
       arr = 1.0 - h[p+1:]
       F[off+dr] += delta * arr
       F[off-dr] -= delta * arr
```
Need ensure `F[off+dl]` indexing with int array works. Yes advanced indexing. But `F[off+dl] += ...` with repeated indices? left dl unique? p - np.arange(p) gives [p,p-1,...,1] unique. right [1..] unique. Good. If p and q updates, some lag indices may overlap (e.g., pair p and q affects two lags), but sequential advanced indexing okay; if same index in one update not repeated. Across updates, add accumulates.

However for p<q, first update p changes F[off+(q-p)] and [off-(q-p)] using h[q] old. Then update q changes same lags using h[p] new? Let's verify pair (p,q) terms. Original terms:
T+ = h_p_old(1-h_q_old), T- = h_q_old(1-h_p_old).
After p to x: T+ = x(1-h_q_old), T- = h_q_old(1-x).
After q to y: T+ = x(1-y), T- = y(1-x).
Second update q uses h[p]=x, and for q update:
 if p<q (p in left of q), d=q-p. update q oldq->y:
  F[off+d] += delta_q * h[:q] includes h[p]=x (since p<q) -> Δ+ = (y-oldq)*x. Final T+ = x(1-hq_old) + (y-hq_old)x = x(y? wait x(1-hq_old) - x(hq_old-y)=x(1-hq_old - hq_old + y)=x(y+1-2hq?) Not right). Let's recompute signs; update_F for q old->new, p<q:

So update function:
```
if p>0:
 dl = p - np.arange(p); left=h[:p]
 F[off+dl] -= delta * left
 F[off-dl] += delta * (1-left)
if p<n-1:
 dr = np.arange(1,n-p); right=h[p+1:]
 F[off+dr] += delta * (1-right)
 F[off-dr] -= delta * right
```

- Then revert p from x to oldp: add_single(h,F,p, old_val=x, new_val=oldp). Uses h[q]=oldq (after revert). Then set h[p]=oldp. This restores.

For applying accepted move, we can leave h updated and F. But during line search, after each trial we revert and keep h at old. At the end, apply best trial (if improvement) by update p/q again. We can store best_x, best_y. Apply once.

Line search details:
```
def line_search_pair(h,F,p,q,dx,beta? M=33):
 n=len(h); off=n-1; L=2*n-1
 oldp=h[p]; oldq=h[q]; s=oldp+oldq
 lo=max(0.0, s-1.0); hi=min(1.0, s)
 if hi-lo < 1e-14: return False
 # generate points: include oldp, lo, hi, maybe linspace M
 xs = unique(np.linspace(lo,hi,M)); xs = np.unique(np.concatenate([xs,[oldp]]))
 best_c = current_c? We can compute current objective maybe global. We can evaluate old? We have F current, current C. To find improvement, evaluate each x; if c < best global? Or local improve.
 for x in xs:
   y=s-x
   # maybe bounds tolerance
   apply p x, q y
   c = max(F)*dx
   if c < best_local: store
   revert
 if improved: apply best
```

- lags involving neither: unchanged.
- pair p-p? k=0 includes both x and s-x quadratics.
- pair p-q: two directed lags ±d with x(s-x?) quadratic (convex) as above.
- lags involving p and another r: affine in x.
- lags involving q and another r: affine in s-x (affine).
Thus objective φ(x)=max(constant floor, quadratic terms (up to 3: k0 concave, p-q two convex), affine terms (up to 2n)). We need min over interval [lo,hi]. This is a 1D problem. def project_feasible(h, n):
   h = np.array(h,dtype=float); clip
   target=n/2.0
   # distribute diff to entries in (0,1), maybe all
   for _ in range(100): ...
   diff=target - np.sum(h)
   if abs(diff)>1e-12:
      # choose index with largest room in direction diff
      if diff>0:
         room = 1.0 - h
         idx = np.argmax(room)
      else: idx = np.argmin(h)
      h[idx] = min(max(h[idx]+diff,0),1)
      diff2=...
      # if still, adjust another
      if abs(diff2)>1e-12:
          # spread remaining
      # final set last? 
   # To force exact: choose an index with h in [0,1] and set to target - sum(h[:-1]) if within [0,1], maybe with np.sum order? We can do for j from n-1 down: if 0<=target-sum(others)<=1 set.
   s = np.sum(h)
   diff=target-s
   if abs(diff)>1e-12:
      # try each index adjust by diff; if possible set
      for idx in range(n):
         val = h[idx] + diff
         if 0 <= val <= 1:
            h[idx]=val
            break
   return h
```

`get_offsets(n)` cache. Use global dict.
```
_OFFSET_CACHE = {}
def get_offsets(n):
  if n not in _OFFSET_CACHE:
     left=[None]*n; right=[None]*n
     for p in range(n):
        left[p] = p - np.arange(p, dtype=int) if p>0 else np.empty(0,dtype=int)
        right[p] = np.arange(1, n-p, dtype=int) if p<n-1 else np.empty(0,dtype=int)
     _OFFSET_CACHE[n]=(p-n+1, left,right)
  return cache[n]
```
Memory for n=512 O(n^2) ~262k ints per side? Actually total n(n-1) ≈262k, 2 arrays? left total n(n-1)/2, right same, total262k ints ~2MB, okay. For multiple n maybe 10MB.

Use `arr = h[:p]` (view). `F[off + left[p]] -= delta * arr`. For subtraction with array, creates temp. Could use `np.add(F_idx, -delta*arr, out=F?)` but advanced indexing out tricky. Fine.

Pair update function:
```
def apply_pair(h,F,p,q,x):
    oldp=h[p]; oldq=h[q]; s=oldp+oldq; y=s-x
    # bound clip x,y; if out skip
    h[p]=x; add_single(h,F,p,oldp,x)
    h[q]=y; add_single(h,F,q,oldq,y)
    return oldp,oldq
```
Revert:
```
def revert_pair(h,F,p,q,oldp,oldq,x,y):
    # current h[p]=x,h[q]=y
    h[q]=oldq; add_single(h,F,q,y,oldq)
    h[p]=oldp; add_single(h,F,p,x,oldp)
```
Wait add_single signature (h,F,p,old,new). To revert q, old=y,new=oldq. It uses h others including p=x. Good. Then set h[q]=oldq. To revert p, old=x,new=oldq? Actually new=oldp. It uses h others including q=oldq. Good.

But note when we set `h[q]=oldq` before `add_single(q,y,oldq)`, does add_single care h[q]? It excludes index q, so okay. But for subsequent p revert, h[q] should be oldq. Good.

```
def coordinate_line_search(h,F,p,q, current_c, best_global, dx, M=33, tol=1e-13, improve_global=False):
    n=len(h); oldp=h[p]; oldq=h[q]; s=oldp+oldq
    lo=max(0.0, s-1.0); hi=min(1.0, s)
    if hi-lo < 1e-14: return False, current_c
    # generate xs: linspace + oldp + maybe midpoints
    if M < 3: ...
    xs = np.linspace(lo, hi, M)
    xs = np.concatenate((xs, [oldp, 0.5*lo+0.5*hi?]))
    # include values near bounds maybe s? if oldp not finite
    xs = xs[(xs>=lo-1e-12)&(xs<=hi+1e-12)]
    xs = np.unique(np.clip(xs,lo,hi))
    best_x=oldp; best_c=current_c
    for x in xs:
       y=s-x
       if y<0 or y>1: continue
       apply_pair; c = np.max(F)*dx
       if c < best_c - 1e-14: best_c=c; best_x=x; best_y=y
       revert_pair
    if best_x != oldp:
       apply best
       return True,best_c
    return False,best_c
```
However each trial reverts and current h restored. But `np.max(F)` after apply may be affected by numerical drift; recompute F occasionally.

Coordinate descent sweep:
```
def coordinate_sweep(h, F, n, steps, p_order, M, max_sweeps, budget, current_c, best_global):
  for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
    if time_left() < 30: break
    improved_sweep=False
    for step in steps:
       for p in p_order:
          q=(p+step)%n
          if p==q: continue
          ok,c = coordinate_line_search(...)
          current_c=c
          if ok: improved_sweep=True; if c<best_global: update best
    # after each full step, recompute F to remove drift
    F = compute_F(h); current_c=max(F)*dx
    if not improved_sweep: break
  return h,F,current_c
```

Binary SA:
```
def binary_make_initial(n, seed, base_h=None):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    a=np.zeros(n)
    # choose random balanced
    idx=np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(idx)
    a[idx[:n//2]]=1
    # optional use base binarization
    if base_h is not None and len(base_h)>0:
       b=binarize(base_h,n)
       if c5(b) < c5(a): a=b
    return a
```

def binarize_continuous(vec, n):

SLSQP/LBFGS polish: We can implement but may not be necessary. However to improve tiny, SLSQP with analytic gradient might help. Let's include a compact polish function using L-BFGS-B penalty, no equality, then project. It can do all-coordinate moves.

`lbfgs_smooth(x, n, beta, mu, maxiter)` using global `_LB_N`, etc. Objective and gradient as described. Need penalty. Let's write top-level:
```
_LB_N=0; _LB_BETA=1.0; _LB_MU=1.0
def lbfgs_fun(x, jac=False):
   n=_LB_N; beta=_LB_BETA; mu=_LB_MU; target=n/2
   dx=2/n
   corr=np.correlate(x,1-x,full)
   m=np.max(corr)
   shifted=beta*(corr-m)
   weights=np.exp(shifted)
   s=weights.sum()
   val=(m + np.log(s)/beta)*dx
   val += mu*(np.sum(x)-target)**2
   if not jac: return val
   # grad corr
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   # weights for lags: p_k/β. Let w_all = weights/s/beta
   w = weights/s/beta
   # k=0 index off
   grad += w[n-1]*(1 - 2*x)
   for d in range(1,n):
      wd = w[n-1+d]
      wmd = w[n-1-d]
      if abs(wd)+abs(wmd) > 1e-18:
         first = n-d
         # first segment 0:first: from Fd (1-x[d:]) and F_-d (-x[d:])
         grad[:first] += (wd - wmd) * (1.0 - x[d:])
         # second segment d:n: from Fd (-x[:first]) and F_-d (1-x[:first])
         grad[d:] += -wd * x[:first] + wmd * (1.0 - x[:first])
   val_grad = dx*grad + 2*mu*(np.sum(x)-target)
   return val, val_grad
```
Check derivative: val = dx * smooth_m; smooth_m derivative has weights p_k/β. We set w = p/β. Then grad_val = dx*grad_c + 2μsumerr. Good.
However correlation computed by `np.correlate` may have indexing different from our F lags? The weights align with `corr` array produced by np.correlate. Our gradient formula assumes corr indices: off=0, off±d as our directed lags. Does np.correlate produce same orientation/order? The max is over all, but weights gradient need correspond to actual corr array from np.correlate. We must ensure our gradient formula matches np.correlate's array ordering. This is critical. We can instead avoid analytic gradient and let L-BFGS finite difference? Too slow. Or use our `compute_F` ordering and objective based on F not np.correlate. But final evaluation uses np.correlate max; if our F max equals np.correlate max, the smooth objective should use our F for consistency. We can make L-BFGS objective based on our `compute_F` (same directed F) not np.correlate. The max over our F equals evaluation max, but the vector order differs. For smooth max, if we optimize over our F, okay as long as max equal. The exact smooth function differs but max objective same. We can compute F using `f_array_from_h` (O(n^2) per eval) instead of correlate; for n<=256 and L-BFGS maybe okay. Then gradient formula as above with our F indexing. This avoids np.correlate orientation. Let's do that. `lbfgs_fun` calls `compute_F(x)` (loop d) O(n^2), plus gradient O(n^2). For n=256 and 100 iterations, ~6M ops, okay. For n=512, ~26M, okay maybe.

But `compute_F` uses Python loop d; 256 iterations per eval, each dot length ~n. L-BFGS maybe 200 evals ->51k vector lengths? Actually 200*256*256=13M, fine. Could be 1s. Good.

Alternatively use `np.correlate` to compute F? Need orientation. Let's determine np.correlate orientation? Could be easier to match, but compute_F safe.

For SLSQP objective, can also use compute_F and smooth max. But L-BFGS penalty enough.

`lbfgs_polish(h,n,beta,mu,maxiter)`:
```
h = h.copy(); target=n/2
project exact? L-BFGS no equality; use penalty.
_LB_N=n; _LB_BETA=beta; _LB_MU=mu
try:
  res = minimize(lbfgs_fun, h, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-7,'maxls':40})
  h2=project_feasible(res.x,n)
except: h2=h
return h2
```
We can call with beta [50,200,1000], mu [1,10,100]. But if mu large, smooth max derivative? Need balance. Use mu=100? Sum error after projection maybe small. Starting h feasible, penalty zero. L-BFGS may move off due bounds, penalty steers. Use mu=10.

Binarize parent:
```
def binarize_from_base(base, n):
  x=interp_to(base, len(base), n)
  target=n//2
  order=np.argsort(-x)
  a=np.zeros(n); a[order[:target]]=1.0
  return a
```
This yields binary sum target. For continuous base fractional, chooses top half, maybe not close? Could use water filling: sort by x descending, assign 1 to top target, 0 rest. Good.

Interp function:
```
def interp_to(src, src_n, dst_n):
 if src_n==dst_n: return src.copy()
 xp=(np.arange(src_n)+0.5)*2.0/src_n
 xn=(np.arange(dst_n)+0.5)*2.0/dst_n
 return np.interp(xn,xp,src)
```

Now binary SA time allocation. The `binary_sa` function should accept `max_iters` or `time_s`. Use time check each iteration maybe every 1000. To avoid time check overhead, loop `while iters >0 and remaining() > time_floor`:
```
def binary_sa(a, seed, time_s, temp_start=5.0, temp_end=0.05, max_iters=None):
  n=len(a); F=compute_F(a); cur=float(np.max(F)); best=...
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  start_t=time.time()
  tt=time_s
  T=temp_start
  it=0
  # if max_iters given limit
  while (max_iters is None or it<max_iters) and time.time()-start_t < tt and remaining()>5:
      p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)
      if a[p]==1 and a[q]==0:
         # apply
         a[p]=0; add_single(a,F,p,1,0)
         a[q]=1; add_single(a,F,q,0,1)
         new=float(np.max(F))
         if new < cur - 1e-12 or (new - cur < T and rng.rand() < math.exp(-(new-cur)/T if new>cur else 0.0)):
             cur=new
             if new*2/n < best_c: update best
         else:
             # revert
             a[q]=0; add_single(a,F,q,1,0)
             a[p]=1; add_single(a,F,p,0,1)
      it+=1
      if it%1000==0:
          F=compute_F(a); cur=float(np.max(F))
          # anneal T
          progress=it/max_iters if max_iters else min(1,(time.time()-start)/tt)
          T=temp_start*(temp_end/temp_start)**progress
  return a, compute_F(a), float(np.max(F))*2/n, best_a,best_c
```
Need if max_iters None use time. The random p,q draw when not suitable still counts iteration? We can loop until suitable but that may waste; better draw in while and if not suitable continue without iter? But count attempts. Use `while attempts < max_attempts` and find suitable; expectation 4. For time, maybe just `p,q = ...; if not suitable: continue` without increment? Could infinite if unbalanced but balanced. We'll use `while attempts < max_attempts and time...` with `attempt+=1`.

def polish_coordinate(h, n, rng, time_s, steps=(1,2,4,8), M=21):
   F=compute_F(h); dx=2.0/n; cur=float(np.max(F))*dx
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur
   start=time.time()
   sweep=0
   while time.time()-start < time_s and remaining()>10:
      improved=False
      order=rng.permutation(n)
      for step in steps:
        for p in order:
          q=(p+step)%n
          if p==q: continue
          ok,new_c = try_coordinate_pair(h,F,p,q,M,cur)
          if ok:
             cur=new_c; improved=True
             if cur < best_c-1e-12: best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
          if time.time()-start > time_s: break
          if remaining()<10: break
        if time to break: break
      # recompute
      F=compute_F(h); cur=max(F)*dx
      if not improved: break
   return best_h,best_c
```

Now L-BFGS analytic gradient based on compute_F. Need ensure compute_F indices match gradient formula. Let's write compute_F carefully indexing:
`F = np.zeros(2n-1); off=n-1; F[off]=sum(h*(1-h))`
For d in 1..n-1:
 `F[off+d] = np.dot(h[:n-d], 1 - h[d:])` (positive shift d: left to right)
 `F[off-d] = np.dot(h[d:], 1 - h[:n-d])` (negative: right to left)
Now gradient formula for F[off+d] and F[off-d] as derived. Let's test with changes single: The gradient at x for F: derivative wrt h_i. For d first segment i=0..n-d-1: for Fd: 1-h[i+d]; for F-d: -h[i+d]. second i=d..n-1: for Fd: -h[i-d]; for F-d: 1-h[i-d]. Yes. In vector:
first len = n-d.
`grad[:first] += wd*(1 - x[d:]) - wmd*x[d:]` (since F_-d derivative -h[d:])
`grad[d:] += -wd*x[:first] + wmd*(1 - x[:first])`
Good.
For d loop, note `x[d:]` length first; `x[:first]` length first. Good.
F0 derivative: 1-2x.

Compute_F used in objective and gradient. This O(n^2) loop. For L-BFGS, objective value uses `F` and smooth. We should not use `np.correlate` in lbfgs. At final, compute_c5 uses correlate, but max should match. There is possible tiny difference due operation order; okay.

SLSQP with analytic gradient and compute_F O(n^2). `minimize` requires `fun` returns float or tuple if `jac=True`? For SLSQP, if `jac=True` passed to minimize, fun should return (f,g). Yes. Constraint dict with `fun`, `jac`. `bounds`. Let's implement.

```
_SQ_N=0; _SQ_BETA=1.0
def sq_obj(x, jac=False):
   val, gradc = smooth_F_and_grad(x, _SQ_BETA)  # returns value Fsmooth, grad_c
   if not jac: return val
   g = gradc
   return val, g
def sq_constraint(x): return np.sum(x) - _SQ_N/2
def sq_jac(x): return np.ones(_SQ_N)
def slsqp_polish(h,n,beta,maxiter):
   set globals; h=project; bounds; res=minimize(sq_obj,h,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':sq_constraint,'jac':sq_jac}],jac=True,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0})
   return project(res.x)
```
But `minimize` with `jac=True` for SLSQP and constraints with jac okay. It may call fun with x and no jac? It uses jac. Good.
Potential problem: SLSQP with 160 variables and equality may have runtime large; maxiter 100. Use after coordinate maybe.

`smooth_F_and_grad(x,beta)` top-level:
```
def smooth_F_and_grad(x, beta):
   F=compute_F(x)
   m=float(np.max(F))
   sh=beta*(F-m)
   e=np.exp(sh)
   s=e.sum()
   val=m + np.log(s)/beta
   w=e/s/beta
   grad=np.zeros_like(x)
   n=len(x); off=n-1
   grad += w[off]*(1-2*x)
   for d in range(1,n):
      wd=w[off+d]; wmd=w[off-d]
      if wd>1e-18 or wmd>1e-18:
         first=n-d
         grad[:first] += (wd - wmd)*(1-x[d:])
         grad[d:] += -wd*x[:first] + wmd*(1-x[:first])
   return val, grad
```

Let's test logic of `project_feasible` exact:
```
def project_feasible(h, n=None):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    if n is None: n=len(h)
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # iterative
    for _ in range(20):
       diff=target-np.sum(h)
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
       if mask.any(): h[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
       else: h+=diff/n
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final exact-ish: adjust one coordinate with enough room
    diff=target-np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       if diff>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1-h))
       else: idx=int(np.argmin(h))
       h[idx]=np.clip(h[idx]+diff,0,1)
    # If still not, distribute
    diff=target-np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       step=diff/n
       h=np.clip(h+step,0,1)
    return h
```
But after final clip, sum may be off. Could use water-filling more robust. Since h in [0,1], target between 0,n, there is always feasible. A simple projection onto simplex with box: Sort? But values already near. The iterative diff to mask works. To make exact, we can do:
```
# set all but last to values, solve last
for idx in range(n-1,-1,-1):
   s_others = np.sum(np.concatenate([h[:idx],h[idx+1:]]))
   val = target - s_others
   if -1e-12 <= val <= 1+1e-12:
       h[idx]=min(1,max(0,val)); break
```
But sum order in evaluator may differ. This ensures that `target - sum_others` is set. If `np.sum(h)` uses pairwise, error small. We can after this return. Good.

Maybe use `math.fsum` to choose exact? Not needed.

`get_offsets`:
```
def get_offsets(n):
    cache = _OFFSET_CACHE
    if n in cache: return cache[n]
    off=n-1
    left=[None]*n; right=[None]*n
    for p in range(n):
       if p>0: left[p]=p - np.arange(p, dtype=int)
       else: left[p]=np.empty(0,dtype=int)
       if p<n-1: right[p]=np.arange(1, n-p, dtype=int)
       else: right[p]=np.empty(0,dtype=int)
    cache[n]=(off,left,right)
    return cache[n]
```
Note left[p] descending; right ascending. Unique.

`add_single`:
```
def add_single(h, F, p, old, new):
    delta = float(new) - float(old)
    if delta == 0.0:
        return
    n = len(h)
    off, left, right = get_offsets(n)
    F[off] += new*(1.0-new) - old*(1.0-old)
    if p > 0:
       dl = left[p]
       hleft = h[:p]
       F[off + dl] -= delta * hleft
       F[off - dl] += delta * (1.0 - hleft)
    rp = right[p]
    if len(rp) > 0:
       hright = h[p+1:]
       F[off + rp] += delta * (1.0 - hright)
       F[off - rp] -= delta * hright
```
Check for p>0 right term: F[off - dl] = lag negative d, term h_p(1-h_q) with changed right p? For q<p, F[-d]=h_p(1-h_q), changed h_p: diff=delta*(1-h_q). We wrote `+= delta*(1-hleft)`. Good. F[+d]=h_q(1-h_p) diff=-delta*h_q. Good.
For right q>p: F[+d]=h_p(1-h_q) diff=delta*(1-hright); F[-d]=h_q(1-h_p) diff=-delta*h_q. Good.

`compute_F`:
```
def compute_F(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    n=len(h); F=np.zeros(2*n-1,float); off=n-1
    F[off]=float(np.sum(h*(1-h)))
    for d in range(1,n):
        a=h[:n-d]; b=h[d:]
        F[off+d]=float(np.dot(a, 1.0-b))
        F[off-d]=float(np.dot(b, 1.0-a))
    return F
```
This may be called often; converting h each time okay. Could avoid if h already array.

def try_coordinate_pair(h, F, p, q, M, cur_fmax):
    if p == q: return False, cur_fmax
    oldp = float(h[p]); oldq = float(h[q]); s = oldp + oldq
    lo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0, s)
    if hi - lo < 1e-14:
        return False, cur_fmax
    if M < 3: M = 3
    xs = np.linspace(lo, hi, M)
    # if oldp not close to any? append
    if abs(oldp - lo) > 1e-12 and abs(oldp - hi) > 1e-12:
        xs = np.concatenate((xs, [oldp]))
    # add maybe midpoint 0.5*(lo+hi)
    mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    if abs(mid - lo)>1e-12 and abs(mid-hi)>1e-12:
        xs = np.concatenate((xs,[mid]))
    xs = np.unique(np.clip(xs, lo, hi))
    best_mf = cur_fmax
    best_x = None
    for x in xs:
       x=float(x)
       if abs(x - oldp) < 1e-14: continue
       y = s - x
       if y < -1e-12 or y > 1.0 + 1e-12: continue
       if y < 0.0: y=0.0
       elif y > 1.0: y=1.0
       # apply
       oldap, oldaq, yy = pair_apply(h,F,p,q,x)
       mf = float(np.max(F))
       pair_revert(h,F,p,q,olda,olda? wait)
```

`polish_coordinate` function:
```
def polish_coordinate(h, n, rng, time_s, steps, M):
    if n <= 1: return h,inf
    h = np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    F = compute_F(h)
    cur = float(np.max(F))
    start = time.time()
    best_h = h.copy(); best_f=cur
    sweep=0
    while time.time()-start < time_s and remaining_time() > 8:
        improved = False
        order = np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(order)
        for step in steps:
            for p in order:
                q = (p + step) % n
                if p == q: continue
                ok, new_f = try_coordinate_pair(h,F,p,q,M,cur)
                if ok:
                    cur = new_f
                    improved = True
                    if cur < best_f - 1e-12:
                        best_f = cur; best_h = h.copy()
                if time.time()-start > time_s or remaining_time() < 8: break
            if time.time()-start > time_s or remaining_time() < 8: break
        # recompute and project
        F = compute_F(h)
        cur = float(np.max(F))
        # project sum
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        F = compute_F(h)
        cur = float(np.max(F))
        if cur < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_f=cur; best_h=h.copy()
        if not improved:
            break
        sweep +=1
    # final best maybe current
    if cur < best_f:
       best_f=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h, best_f* (2.0/n)
```
Need ensure `project_feasible` changes h, but then F recompute. If project adjusts, current may change. If projecting best_h? We only project current; best_h copy before project. If project improves current, update. Good.

Potential issue: `project_feasible` may change h significantly if sum drifted, breaking local optimum; okay.

def make_steps(n):
  base=[1,2,3,5,8,13,21]
  if n//2>1: base.append(n//2)
  out=[]
  for s in base:
    s%=n
    if s not in out: out.append(s)
  return tuple(out[:6])
```

`ensure_binary_balanced`:
```
def make_balanced_binary(a, seed):
    n=len(a); target=n//2
    b=np.zeros(n)
    if np.all((a<=1)&(a>=0)):
       order=np.argsort(-a)
       b[order[:target]]=1.0
    # if sum already target and binary, use as is
    if abs(np.sum(a)-target)<1e-12 and np.all((a==0)|(a==1)): return a
    return b
```
But if input binary not balanced, this reorders by values (all 1/0) arbitrary. Good.

`binary_sa`:
```
def binary_sa(a, seed, time_s, Tstart=2.0, Tend=0.05):
    n=len(a)
    a=make_balanced_binary(a, np.random.RandomState(seed))
    F=compute_F(a)
    cur=float(np.max(F)); best_c=cur*2.0/n; best_a=a.copy()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed+1)
    start=time.time(); elapsed=0
    it=0; T=Tstart
    while time.time()-start < time_s and remaining_time() > 3:
       # draw suitable pair; cap attempts
       p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)
       ap=a[p]; aq=a[q]
       if not (ap>0.5 and aq<0.5):
          # maybe choose by nonzero arrays every 1000? Just continue but count? To avoid infinite, if many fails, use nonzero
          if it % 64 == 0:
             ones=np.nonzero(a>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(a<0.5)[0]
             if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: break
             p=int(rng.choice(ones)); q=int(rng.choice(zeros))
       # apply
       a[p]=0.0; add_single(a,F,p,1.0,0.0)
       a[q]=1.0; add_single(a,F,q,0.0,1.0)
       new=float(np.max(F))
       if new < cur - 1e-9:
           cur=new; accept=True
       elif abs(new-cur) < 1e-9:
           accept = (rng.rand() < 0.8)
       else:
           diff=new-cur
           accept = False

But `continue` without updating it? It consumes time. Fine. Let's implement using a helper `random_swap_indices(a,rng)`:
```
def random_swap_indices(a, rng):
   n=len(a)
   for _ in range(8):
      p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
      if a[p] > 0.5 and a[q] < 0.5: return p,q
   ones=np.nonzero(a>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(a<0.5)[0]
   if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: return None
   return int(rng.choice(ones)), int(rng.choice(zeros))
```
This top-level. In SA, call each iteration; overhead okay.

Let's write `lbfgs_polish`:
```
def lbfgs_polish(h, n, beta, mu, maxiter):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h,dtype=float), n)
    _LB_N=n; _LB_BETA=float(beta); _LB_MU=float(mu)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
        res = minimize(lbfgs_fun, h, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds,
                       options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-14, 'gtol': 1e-7, 'maxls': 40, 'disp': False})
        h2 = project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        h2 = h
    return h2
```
`lbfgs_fun`:
```
def lbfgs_fun(x, jac=False):
    n=_LB_N; beta=_LB_BETA; mu=_LB_MU
    val, gradF = smooth_F_grad(x, beta)
    dx=2.0/n
    val = val*dx
    err = float(np.sum(x) - n/2.0)
    val += 0.5*mu*err*err
    if not jac: return val
    g = gradF*dx + mu*err*np.ones(n)
    return val, g
```
`smooth_F_grad` returns smooth F value (not dx) and grad of smooth F.
Implementation stable:
```
def smooth_F_grad(x,beta):
    F=compute_F(x)
    m=float(np.max(F))
    sh=beta*(F-m)
    e=np.exp(sh)
    s=float(e.sum())
    val=m + np.log(s)/beta
    w=e/(s*beta)
    n=len(x); off=n-1
    grad=np.zeros(n,float)
    grad += w[off]*(1.0-2.0*x)
    for d in range(1,n):
       wd=w[off+d]; wmd=w[off-d]
       if wd > 1e-16 or wmd > 1e-16:
           first=n-d
           grad[:first] += (wd - wmd)*(1.0 - x[d:])
           grad[d:] += -wd*x[:first] + wmd*(1.0 - x[:first])
    val,? also need val smooth without dx. But val computed as m+log(s)/beta; note if m large positive, m + log(s)/beta? Since sh=beta(F-m), log s ≤0? Actually log s <=0, so val = m + log(s)/beta less than m? Wait logsumexp(beta(F-m)) = log(s), but m + log(s)/beta is correct (≤m). Good.
    return val, grad
```
If beta very high, w for max =1/β if one active; grad fine. For m maybe negative? F values nonnegative? h(1-h) nonnegative, yes. m≥0. Good.

SLSQP `sq_obj`:
```
def sq_obj(x, jac=False):
    val, gradF = smooth_F_grad(x, _SQ_BETA)
    dx=2.0/_SQ_N
    if not jac: return val*dx
    return val*dx, gradF*dx
def sq_con(x): return float(np.sum(x) - _SQ_N/2.0)
def sq_con_jac(x): return np.ones(_SQ_N)
def slsqp_polish(h,n,beta,maxiter):
    h=project_feasible(...)
    set globals
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sq_con,'jac':sq_con_jac}]
    res=minimize(sq_obj,h,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,jac=True,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0})
    return project_feasible(res.x,n)
```
SLSQP may call obj with `x` and require constraint jac shape (1,n)? If `jac` returns vector? For scalar constraint, Jacobian is 1-D array length n acceptable? I think yes. Could return `np.ones((1,_SQ_N))`? SLSQP accepts either. Use `np.ones(_SQ_N)`.

def de_obj(x):
    n=_DE_N; dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full')
    c=float(np.max(corr))*dx
    err=float(np.sum(x)-_DE_TARGET)
    return c + _DE_MU*err*err
```

def run_de(n, x0, seed, popsize, maxiter):

    _DE_N=n; _DE_TARGET=n/2.0; _DE_MU=100.0
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n

    try:
       res = differential_evolution(de_obj, bounds=bounds, x0=x0, maxiter=int(maxiter), popsize=float(popsize), tol=1e-8, init='latinhypercube', polish=False, seed=int(seed), updating='inplace')
       return project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
       return None
```

`make_steps`:
```
def make_steps(n):
    base=[1,2,3,5,8,13,21]
    if n//2 >1: base.append(n//2)
    out=[]
    for s in base:
       s=int(s)%n
       if s!=0 and s not in out:
          out.append(s)
    if len(out)>6: out=out[:6]
    return tuple(out)
```

Now let's prove gradient sign one more time using a simple change. n=3 h=[0,0,1], F values above. Compute smooth gradient at beta infinite active max lags F1=1 (idx1) and? F0? Actually max=1 at idx1? and no other? F[1]=1, others0. For active lag -1 (off-1 index1), weight 1/β. Our formula for d=1: wd=w[3]=0, wmd=w[1]=1/β. first=2.

F0 grad +=0. Total grad = [-1/β, 0, 1/β]. Let's manually derivative of F_-1 = h1(1-h0)+h2(1-h1) =0+1=1. ∂/∂h0 = -h1=0, ∂/∂h1=1-h0=1, ∂/∂h2=-h1=0. Wait our gradient [-1/β,0,1/β] wrong? I previously set F_-d derivative second segment = 1-h[j-d]. So for h2 (i=2), derivative 1-h1=1. Good. For h1 (i=1), derivative 1-h0=1; but h1 also appears in first segment? For F_-d, h1 appears in first segment? F_-d term for i=1 is h1(1-h0): derivative wrt h1 = 1-h0=1. Wait our decomposition for first/second segments double counts? Let's align indices:
For F_d = ∑_{i=0}^{n-d-1} h_i(1-h_{i+d}).
For F_-d = ∑_{i=d}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-d}).
Derivatives wrt coordinate j:
- If j < d: appears only in F_d? For F_d: as left i=j if j≤n-d-1 => deriv 1-h_{j+d}. As right in F_d: if j≥d? no. In F_-d: as right? if j-d? F_-d term for pair j-d<j: h_j(1-h_{j-d})? That's j as left in F_-d? Actually F_-d left index i runs d..n-1. If j ≥ d, appears as h_j; if j<n-d, appears as h_{j-d}? Let's do case j<d: j=0, d=1.
F_d: h0 term -> deriv 1-h1=1 (since h1=0). F_-d: h2(1-h1) no h0. So ∂ total for Fd+F_-d? Weights separate. For wmd only (F_-d), derivative 0. For wd, derivative 1. Our combined formula for first segment (j=0) in `smooth_F_grad`: `grad[:first] += (wd - wmd)*(1-x[d:])`. For wd=0,wmd=1/β => -(1/β)*1 = -1/β. But actual derivative of F_-d wrt h0 should? F_-d = h1(1-h0)+h2(1-h1); derivative wrt h0 = -h1=0! So our formula for F_-d first segment as `+ wmd*(1-x[d:])` is wrong? Let's re-evaluate derivative of F_-d wrt first segment (i=0..n-d-1). F_-d = ∑_{k=0}^{n-d-1} h_{k+d}(1-h_k). Here first segment index k is the lower index, but F_-d term has (1-h_k), so derivative wrt h_k is -h_{k+d}, NOT (1-h_{k+d}). I mistakenly used sign. For F_d: term h_k(1-h_{k+d}), derivative wrt h_k = 1 - h_{k+d}. For F_-d: term h_{k+d}(1-h_k), derivative wrt h_k = -h_{k+d}. So first segment gradient = wd*(1 - x[d:]) - wmd*x[d:]. That's what I wrote in grad? I wrote `grad[:first] += (wd - wmd)*(1.0 - x[d:])` in last, but earlier derived `wd*(1-x[d:]) + wmd*(-x[d:]) = wd(1-x[d:]) - wmd*x[d:]`, not `(wd-wmd)*(1-x[d:])`. Correct formula: `wd*(1 - x[d:]) - wmd*x[d:]`. For second segment j=d..n-1, index k=j-d: F_d term h_k(1-h_j) derivative wrt h_j = -h_k = -x[:first]. F_-d term h_j(1-h_k) derivative wrt h_j = 1 - h_k = (1-x[:first]). So second = -wd*x[:first] + wmd*(1-x[:first]). That part was correct.

Thus `smooth_F_grad` first line should be:
`grad[:first] += wd*(1.0 - x[d:]) - wmd*x[d:]`
Let's test with wmd=1: grad0=-x1=0, grad1=-x2? For j=1 (still first segment? first=2 includes j=1): derivative F_-d wrt h1 from F_-d term h1(1-h0) (as left) =1-h0=1? Wait h1 is also second segment? d=1, j=1 ≥d and j<d? It is both first segment index k=1? first=2 includes i=0,1 for F_d. For F_-d, j=1 appears as left of term h1(1-h0): derivative 1-h0=1. In our decomposition first segment k=1: F_self? For F_-d term h_{1+d}=h2(1-h1): derivative wrt h1 = -h2 = -1; this is as right. Plus F_d term h1(1-h2) derivative wrt h1 = 1-h2=0 (w d=0). Total for F_-d wrt h1 = 1 (as left) + (-1) (as right) =0. Our first segment formula for j=1: -wmd*x[2] = -1, second segment formula for j=1 (k=0): wmd*(1-x[0]) =1, total0. Good. For j=2 second only: wmd*(1-x[1])=1. Total gradient [0,0,1]. Matches manual. Good.
So fix gradient formula.

Let's update `smooth_F_grad`:
```
grad[:first] += wd*(1.0 - x[d:]) - wmd*x[d:]
grad[d:] += -wd*x[:first] + wmd*(1.0 - x[:first])
```

`make_balanced_binary(a, seed)`:
```
def make_balanced_binary(a, seed=None):
   n=len(a)
   target=n//2 if n%2==0 else int(round(n/2.0))? For odd? Binary sum target n/2 not integer if odd. But we only call even n. Use target=int(round(n/2)).
   arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
   if n==0: return arr
   # if already binary balanced
   if n%2==0 and abs(np.sum(arr)-target)<1e-10 and np.all((arr<0.5)">? ) maybe if values 0/1.
   Top target by arr values.
   order=np.argsort(-arr, kind='mergesort')
   b=np.zeros(n,float); b[order[:target]]=1.0
   return b
```
For continuous, chooses top half. If parent has fractional 0.5 ties arbitrary. Fine.

`random_swap_indices(a,rng)`:
```
def random_swap_indices(a, rng):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return (-1,-1)
    for _ in range(8):
       p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
       if p!=q and a[p] > 0.5 and a[q] < 0.5:
          return p,q
    ones=np.nonzero(a > 0.5)[0]
    zeros=np.nonzero(a < 0.5)[0]
    if len(ones)==0 or len(zeros)==0: return (-1,-1)
    return (int(rng.choice(ones)), int(rng.choice(zeros)))
```
For binary after many moves sum fixed half, both nonempty unless all? If n even target n/2, okay.

`binary_sa` details:
```
def binary_sa(a, seed, time_s, Tstart=2.5, Tend=0.05):
    n=len(a)
    a=make_balanced_binary(a, seed)
    F=compute_F(a)
    cur=float(np.max(F))
    best_c=cur*2.0/n
    best_a=a.copy()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    start=time.time()
    it=0; T=float(Tstart); fails=0
    while time.time()-start < float(time_s) and remaining_time() > 3:
       p,q=random_swap_indices(a,rng)
       if p<0: break
       # apply
       a[p]=0.0; add_single(a,F,p,1.0,0.0)
       a[q]=1.0; add_single(a,F,q,0.0,1.0)
       new=float(np.max(F))
       accept=False
       if new < cur - 1e-10:
          accept=True
       elif abs(new-cur) <= 1e-10:
          accept = (rng.rand() < 0.75)
       else:
          diff=new-cur
          if diff <= T:
             # compute prob
             accept = (rng.rand() < math.exp(-diff / T if T > 1e-12 else 0.0))
       if accept:
          cur = new
          c = cur*2.0/n
          if c < best_c - 1e-12:
             best_c=c; best_a=a.copy()
       else:
          # revert
          a[q]=0.0; add_single(a,F,q,1.0,0.0)
          a[p]=1.0; add_single(a,F,p,0.0,1.0)
       it+=1
       if it % 2000 == 0:
          F=compute_F(a); cur=float(np.max(F)); c=cur*2.0/n
          if c < best_c - 1e-12:
             best_c=c; best_a=a.copy()
          elapsed=time.time()-start
          prog = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-9, time_s))
          T = Tstart * (Tend/Tstart) ** prog
    return best_a, best_c
```
If time_s=0, while false? `time_s` conversion. If time_s <=0, skip? In run call with min maybe 0; ensure:
```
if time_s <=0: return make_balanced_binary, compute_c5
```

`polish_coordinate` needs `steps` tuple and M. Add if time_s<=0 return h,c.
```
def polish_coordinate(h,n,rng,time_s,steps,M=15):
   h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
   F=compute_F(h); cur=float(np.max(F)); c=cur*2/n
   if time_s <=0: return h,c
   start=time.time(); changed=0; best_h=h.copy(); best_f=cur
   while ...:
      improved=False; order=np.array(np.arange(n)); rng.shuffle(order)
      for step in steps:
         for p in order:
            q=(p+step)%n
            if p==q: continue
            ok,new_f=try_coordinate_pair(h,F,p,q,int(M),cur)
            if ok:
               cur=new_f; improved=True; changed+=1
               if changed >= 50:
                  F=compute_F(h); cur=float(np.max(F)); changed=0
                  if cur < best_f: update
            if time to break: break
         if ... break
      F=compute_F(h); h=project_feasible(h,n); F=compute_F(h); cur=max
      if cur < best_f: update
      if not improved: break
   if cur<best_f: update
   return best_h, best_f*2/n
```

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0: return h
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(30):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15: break
        mask = (h > 1e-14) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-14)
        cnt = int(np.sum(mask))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[mask] += diff / cnt
        else:
            h[:] += diff / n
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    # single final adjustment at an internal value
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
        val = h[idx] + diff
        if 0.0 <= val <= 1.0:
            h[idx] = val
    # if still, spread tiny
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        h = np.clip(h + diff / n, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```

def make_balanced_binary(a, seed=None):
   arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
   n=len(arr); target=n//2
   if seed is not None:
      arr = arr + 1e-7*np.random.RandomState(seed).rand(n)
   order=np.argsort(-arr, kind='mergesort')
   b=np.zeros(n,float); b[order[:target]]=1.0
   return b
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution

_START_TIME = 0.0
_BUDGET_S = 1300.0
_OFFSET_CACHE = {}

_LB_N = 0
_LB_BETA = 1.0
_LB_MU = 1.0

_SQ_N = 0
_SQ_BETA = 1.0

_DE_N = 0
_DE_TARGET = 0.0
_DE_MU = 100.0


def remaining_time():
    return max(0.0, _BUDGET_S - (time.time() - _START_TIME) - 2.0)


def get_offsets(n):
    if n in _OFFSET_CACHE:
        return _OFFSET_CACHE[n]
    off = n - 1
    left = [None] * n
    right = [None] * n
    for p in range(n):
        if p > 0:
            left[p] = p - np.arange(p, dtype=int)
        else:
            left[p] = np.empty(0, dtype=int)
        if p < n - 1:
            right[p] = np.arange(1, n - p, dtype=int)
        else:
            right[p] = np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    _OFFSET_CACHE[n] = (off, left, right)
    return _OFFSET_CACHE[n]


def project_feasible(h, n=None):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if n is None:
        n = len(h)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return h
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(40):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        mask = (h > 1e-14) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-14)
        cnt = int(np.sum(mask))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
        else:
            h[:] += diff / float(n)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
        val = h[idx] + diff
        if 0.0 <= val <= 1.0:
            h[idx] = val

    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        h = np.clip(h + diff / float(n), 0.0, 1.0)

    return h


def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr)) * dx


def compute_F(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    off = n - 1
    F = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    F[off] = float(np.sum(h * (1.0 - h)))
    for d in range(1, n):
        a = h[:n - d]
        b = h[d:]
        F[off + d] = float(np.dot(a, 1.0 - b))
        F[off - d] = float(np.dot(b, 1.0 - a))
    return F


def add_single(h, F, p, old, new):
    delta = float(new) - float(old)
    if delta == 0.0:
        return
    n = len(h)
    off, left, right = get_offsets(n)

    F[off] += new * (1.0 - new) - old * (1.0 - old)

    if p > 0:
        dl = left[p]
        hleft = h[:p]
        F[off + dl] -= delta * hleft
        F[off - dl] += delta * (1.0 - hleft)

    rp = right[p]
    if len(rp) > 0:
        hright = h[p + 1:]
        F[off + rp] += delta * (1.0 - hright)
        F[off - rp] -= delta * hright


def pair_apply(h, F, p, q, x, y):
    oldp = float(h[p])
    oldq = float(h[q])
    h[p] = x
    add_single(h, F, p, oldp, x)
    h[q] = y
    add_single(h, F, q, oldq, y)
    return oldp, oldq


def pair_revert(h, F, p, q, oldp, oldq, x, y):
    h[q] = oldq
    add_single(h, F, q, y, oldq)
    h[p] = oldp
    add_single(h, F, p, x, oldp)


def try_coordinate_pair(h, F, p, q, M, cur_fmax):
    if p == q:
        return False, cur_fmax

    oldp = float(h[p])
    oldq = float(h[q])
    s = oldp + oldq
    lo = max(0.0, s - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0, s)
    if hi - lo < 1e-14:
        return False, cur_fmax

    if M < 3:
        M = 3
    xs = np.linspace(lo, hi, int(M))
    if abs(oldp - lo) > 1e-12 and abs(oldp - hi) > 1e-12:
        xs = np.concatenate((xs, [oldp]))
    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    if abs(mid - lo) > 1e-12 and abs(mid - hi) > 1e-12:
        xs = np.concatenate((xs, [mid]))
    xs = np.unique(np.clip(xs, lo, hi))

    best_mf = cur_fmax
    best_x = None
    best_y = None

    for x in xs:
        x = float(x)
        if abs(x - oldp) < 1e-14:
            continue
        y = s - x
        if y < -1e-12 or y > 1.0 + 1e-12:
            continue
        if y < 0.0:
            y = 0.0
        elif y > 1.0:
            y = 1.0

        pair_apply(h, F, p, q, x, y)
        mf = float(np.max(F))
        pair_revert(h, F, p, q, oldp, oldq, x, y)

        if mf < best_mf - 1e-12:
            best_mf = mf
            best_x = x
            best_y = y

    if best_x is not None:
        pair_apply(h, F, p, q, float(best_x), float(best_y))
        return True, float(best_mf)
    return False, cur_fmax


def interp_to(src, src_n, dst_n):
    src_n = int(src_n)
    dst_n = int(dst_n)
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=float)
    if src_n == dst_n:
        return src.copy()
    xp = (np.arange(src_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(src_n))
    xnew = (np.arange(dst_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(dst_n))
    return np.interp(xnew, xp, src)


def polish_coordinate(h, n, rng, time_s, steps, M=15):
    n = int(n)
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    F = compute_F(h)
    cur_f = float(np.max(F))
    cur_c = cur_f * (2.0 / float(n))
    if time_s is None or float(time_s) <= 0.0:
        return h, cur_c

    start = time.time()
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_f = cur_f
    changed = 0

    while time.time() - start < float(time_s) and remaining_time() > 8:
        improved = False
        order = np.arange(n)
        rng.shuffle(order)

        for step in steps:
            for p in order:
                q = (int(p) + int(step)) % n
                if p == q:
                    continue
                ok, new_f = try_coordinate_pair(h, F, int(p), int(q), int(M), cur_f)
                if ok:
                    cur_f = float(new_f)
                    improved = True
                    changed += 1
                    if cur_f < best_f - 1e-12:
                        best_f = cur_f
                        best_h = h.copy()
                    if changed >= 50:
                        F = compute_F(h)
                        cur_f = float(np.max(F))
                        changed = 0
                        if cur_f < best_f - 1e-12:
                            best_f = cur_f
                            best_h = h.copy()
                if time.time() - start > float(time_s) or remaining_time() < 8:
                    break
            if time.time() - start > float(time_s) or remaining_time() < 8:
                break

        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        F = compute_F(h)
        cur_f = float(np.max(F))
        if cur_f < best_f - 1e-12:
            best_f = cur_f
            best_h = h.copy()

        if not improved:
            break

    if cur_f < best_f:
        best_f = cur_f
        best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, float(best_f) * (2.0 / float(n))


def make_steps(n):
    n = int(n)
    base = [1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, 21]
    if n // 2 > 1:
        base.append(n // 2)
    out = []
    for s in base:
        s = int(s) % n
        if s != 0 and s not in out:
            out.append(s)
    if len(out) > 5:
        out = out[:5]
    return tuple(out)


def make_balanced_binary(a, seed=None):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = len(arr)
    if n == 0:
        return arr.copy()
    if n % 2 != 0:
        target = int(round(0.5 * n))
    else:
        target = n // 2
    work = arr.copy()
    if seed is not None:
        work = work + 1e-7 * np.random.RandomState(int(seed)).rand(n)
    order = np.argsort(-work, kind="mergesort")
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    b[order[:target]] = 1.0
    return b


def random_swap_indices(a, rng):
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 1:
        return -1, -1
    for _ in range(8):
        p = int(rng.randint(n))
        q = int(rng.randint(n))
        if p != q and a[p] > 0.5 and a[q] < 0.5:
            return p, q
    ones = np.nonzero(a > 0.5)[0]
    zeros = np.nonzero(a < 0.5)[0]
    if len(ones) == 0 or len(zeros) == 0:
        return -1, -1
    return int(rng.choice(ones)), int(rng.choice(zeros))


def binary_sa(a, seed, time_s, Tstart=2.5, Tend=0.05):
    a = make_balanced_binary(a, None)
    n = len(a)
    F = compute_F(a)
    cur_f = float(np.max(F))
    best_c = cur_f * (2.0 / float(n))
    best_a = a.copy()
    if n <= 1 or time_s is None or float(time_s) <= 0.0:
        return best_a, best_c

    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    start = time.time()
    it = 0
    T = float(Tstart)

    while time.time() - start < float(time_s) and remaining_time() > 3:
        p, q = random_swap_indices(a, rng)
        if p < 0:
            break

        a[p] = 0.0
        add_single(a, F, p, 1.0, 0.0)
        a[q] = 1.0
        add_single(a, F, q, 0.0, 1.0)
        new_f = float(np.max(F))

        accept = False
        if new_f < cur_f - 1e-10:
            accept = True
        elif abs(new_f - cur_f) <= 1e-10:
            accept = bool(rng.rand() < 0.75)
        else:
            diff = new_f - cur_f
            if diff <= T:
                accept = bool(rng.rand() < math.exp(-diff / max(T, 1e-12)))

        if accept:
            cur_f = new_f
            c = cur_f * (2.0 / float(n))
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c
                best_a = a.copy()
        else:
            a[q] = 0.0
            add_single(a, F, q, 1.0, 0.0)
            a[p] = 1.0
            add_single(a, F, p, 0.0, 1.0)

        it += 1
        if it % 2000 == 0:
            F = compute_F(a)
            cur_f = float(np.max(F))
            c = cur_f * (2.0 / float(n))
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c
                best_a = a.copy()
            elapsed = time.time() - start
            prog = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-9, float(time_s)))
            T = float(Tstart) * (float(Tend) / float(Tstart)) ** prog

    return best_a, best_c


def smooth_F_grad(x, beta):
    F = compute_F(x)
    n = len(x)
    m = float(np.max(F))
    shifted = float(beta) * (F - m)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(e.sum())
    val = m + math.log(s) / float(beta)

    w = e / (s * float(beta))
    off = n - 1
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    grad += w[off] * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
    for d in range(1, n):
        wd = float(w[off + d])
        wmd = float(w[off - d])
        if wd > 1e-16 or wmd > 1e-16:
            first = n - d
            grad[:first] += wd * (1.0 - x[d:]) - wmd * x[d:]
            grad[d:] += -wd * x[:first] + wmd * (1.0 - x[:first])

    return val, grad


def lbfgs_fun(x, jac=False):
    n = int(_LB_N)
    beta = float(_LB_BETA)
    mu = float(_LB_MU)
    valF, gradF = smooth_F_grad(x, beta)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    val = valF * dx
    err = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(n))
    val += 0.5 * mu * err * err
    if not jac:
        return val
    g = gradF * dx + mu * err * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    return val, g


def lbfgs_polish(h, n, beta, mu, maxiter):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h, dtype=float), n)
    n = int(n)
    global _LB_N, _LB_BETA, _LB_MU
    _LB_N = n
    _LB_BETA = float(beta)
    _LB_MU = float(mu)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lbfgs_fun,
            h,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxls": 40,
                "disp": False,
            },
        )
        return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        return h


def sq_obj(x, jac=False):
    valF, gradF = smooth_F_grad(x, float(_SQ_BETA))
    dx = 2.0 / float(_SQ_N)
    if not jac:
        return valF * dx
    return valF * dx, gradF * dx


def sq_con(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(_SQ_N))


def sq_con_jac(x):
    return np.ones((1, int(_SQ_N)), dtype=float)


def slsqp_polish(h, n, beta, maxiter):
    h = project_feasible(np.array(h, dtype=float), n)
    n = int(n)
    global _SQ_N, _SQ_BETA
    _SQ_N = n
    _SQ_BETA = float(beta)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": sq_con,
            "jac": sq_con_jac,
        }
    ]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            sq_obj,
            h,
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "iprint": 0,
            },
        )
        return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        return h


def de_obj(x):
    n = int(_DE_N)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    c = float(np.max(corr)) * dx
    err = float(np.sum(x) - float(_DE_TARGET))
    return c + float(_DE_MU) * err * err


def run_de(n, x0, seed, popsize, maxiter):
    global _DE_N, _DE_TARGET, _DE_MU
    n = int(n)
    _DE_N = n
    _DE_TARGET = 0.5 * float(n)
    _DE_MU = 100.0
    x0 = project_feasible(np.array(x0, dtype=float), n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = differential_evolution(
            de_obj,
            bounds,
            x0=x0,
            maxiter=int(maxiter),
            popsize=float(popsize),
            tol=1e-8,
            polish=False,
            seed=int(seed),
        )
        return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        return None


def make_default_start(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    h = rng.rand(int(n))
    return project_feasible(h, int(n))


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _START_TIME, _BUDGET_S
    _START_TIME = time.time()
    _BUDGET_S = float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    best_h = None
    best_n = 0
    best_c5 = float("inf")

    try:
        ph = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        n0 = len(ph)
        if n0 == 0:
            raise ValueError("empty parent")
        ph = project_feasible(ph, n0)
        best_h = ph
        best_n = n0
        best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)
    except Exception:
        best_n = 160
        best_h = make_default_start(best_n, seed)
        best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)

    if best_n <= 0 or (best_n % 2) != 0:
        if best_n > 0:
            new_n = best_n + 1 if (best_n % 2) == 1 else 160
        else:
            new_n = 160
        if best_n > 0:
            best_h = interp_to(best_h, best_n, new_n)
        else:
            best_h = make_default_start(new_n, seed)
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, new_n)
        best_n = new_n
        best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)

    parent_n = best_n

    if remaining_time() > 250:
        de_configs = [(64, 6, 40), (80, 5, 30), (128, 4, 25)]
        for n_de, pop, iters in de_configs:
            if remaining_time() < 80:
                break
            x0 = interp_to(best_h, best_n, n_de)
            x0 = project_feasible(x0, n_de)
            hd = run_de(n_de, x0, seed + n_de, pop, iters)
            if hd is not None:
                cd = compute_c5(hd)
                if cd < best_c5:
                    best_c5 = cd
                    best_h = hd.copy()
                    best_n = n_de

    small_configs = [(64, 15), (80, 15), (96, 15), (128, 20)]
    for n_small, sa_t in small_configs:
        if remaining_time() < 70:
            break
        hbase = interp_to(best_h, best_n, n_small)
        hbase = project_feasible(hbase, n_small)
        cbase = compute_c5(hbase)
        if cbase < best_c5:
            best_c5 = cbase
            best_h = hbase.copy()
            best_n = n_small

        hb = make_balanced_binary(hbase, seed + n_small)
        if remaining_time() > 50:
            sa_a, sa_c = binary_sa(
                hb, seed + n_small + 7, min(float(sa_t), remaining_time() * 0.15), 2.5, 0.05
            )
            if sa_c < best_c5:
                best_c5 = sa_c
                best_h = sa_a.copy()
                best_n = n_small

        if (cbase < best_c5 + 0.002 or cbase < 0.3815) and remaining_time() > 70:
            hp, pc = polish_coordinate(
                hbase, n_small, rng, min(20.0, remaining_time() * 0.10), make_steps(n_small), 13
            )
            if pc < best_c5:
                best_c5 = pc
                best_h = hp.copy()
                best_n = n_small

        if sa_c < best_c5 + 0.002 and remaining_time() > 60:
            hp, pc = polish_coordinate(
                sa_a, n_small, rng, min(15.0, remaining_time() * 0.10), make_steps(n_small), 13
            )
            if pc < best_c5:
                best_c5 = pc
                best_h = hp.copy()
                best_n = n_small

    n_main = parent_n
    if remaining_time() > 250:
        hmain = interp_to(best_h, best_n, n_main)
        hmain = project_feasible(hmain, n_main)
        cmain = compute_c5(hmain)
        if cmain < best_c5:
            best_c5 = cmain
            best_h = hmain.copy()
            best_n = n_main

        steps_main = make_steps(n_main)
        t_cd = min(180.0, remaining_time() * 0.35)
        hp, pc = polish_coordinate(hmain, n_main, rng, t_cd, steps_main, 15)
        if pc < best_c5:
            best_c5 = pc
            best_h = hp.copy()
            best_n = n_main

        if remaining_time() > 120:
            hb = make_balanced_binary(hmain, seed + n_main + 101)
            t_sa = min(120.0, remaining_time() * 0.30)
            sa_a, sa_c = binary_sa(hb, seed + n_main + 113, t_sa, 2.5, 0.05)
            if sa_c < best_c5:
                best_c5 = sa_c
                best_h = sa_a.copy()
                best_n = n_main
            if sa_c < best_c5 + 0.002 and remaining_time() > 80:
                hp2, pc2 = polish_coordinate(
                    sa_a, n_main, rng, min(80.0, remaining_time() * 0.20), steps_main, 15
                )
                if pc2 < best_c5:
                    best_c5 = pc2
                    best_h = hp2.copy()
                    best_n = n_main

    if remaining_time() > 150:
        for beta in [30.0, 100.0, 300.0]:
            if remaining_time() < 40:
                break
            hl = lbfgs_polish(best_h, best_n, beta, 20.0, 80)
            cl = compute_c5(hl)
            if cl < best_c5:
                best_c5 = cl
                best_h = hl.copy()

        if remaining_time() > 60:
            hs = slsqp_polish(best_h, best_n, 100.0, 80)
            cs = compute_c5(hs)
            if cs < best_c5:
                best_c5 = cs
                best_h = hs.copy()

    other_ns = []
    for n in [best_n - 32, best_n + 32, 192, 256, 320, 128]:
        if n not in other_ns and 64 <= n <= 512 and (n % 2) == 0 and n != best_n:
            other_ns.append(n)

    for n_other in other_ns:
        if remaining_time() < 80:
            break
        h = interp_to(best_h, best_n, n_other)
        h = project_feasible(h, n_other)
        c0 = compute_c5(h)
        if c0 < best_c5:
            best_c5 = c0
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_n = n_other

        t_cd = min(30.0, remaining_time() * 0.20)
        if t_cd > 5.0:
            hp, pc = polish_coordinate(h, n_other, rng, t_cd, make_steps(n_other), 13)
            if pc < best_c5:
                best_c5 = pc
                best_h = hp.copy()
                best_n = n_other

        if remaining_time() > 50 and c0 < best_c5 + 0.002:
            hb = make_balanced_binary(h, seed + n_other + 331)
            sa_a, sa_c = binary_sa(
                hb, seed + n_other + 337, min(20.0, remaining_time() * 0.15), 2.5, 0.05
            )
            if sa_c < best_c5:
                best_c5 = sa_c
                best_h = sa_a.copy()
                best_n = n_other

    if remaining_time() > 70:
        h_rev = best_h[::-1].copy()
        c_rev = compute_c5(h_rev)
        if c_rev < best_c5:
            best_c5 = c_rev
            best_h = h_rev.copy()

        h_sym = 0.5 * (best_h + (1.0 - best_h[::-1]))
        h_sym = project_feasible(h_sym, best_n)
        c_sym = compute_c5(h_sym)
        if c_sym < best_c5:
            best_c5 = c_sym
            best_h = h_sym.copy()

        if remaining_time() > 40:
            hp, pc = polish_coordinate(
                h_sym, best_n, rng, min(35.0, remaining_time() * 0.35), make_steps(best_n), 15
            )
            if pc < best_c5:
                best_c5 = pc
                best_h = hp.copy()

    if remaining_time() > 60:
        for k in range(3):
            if remaining_time() < 25:
                break
            scale = 0.02 + 0.015 * float(k)
            h2 = best_h + scale * rng.randn(best_n)
            h2 = project_feasible(h2, best_n)
            hp, pc = polish_coordinate(
                h2, best_n, rng, min(18.0, remaining_time() * 0.20), make_steps(best_n), 13
            )
            if pc < best_c5:
                best_c5 = pc
                best_h = hp.copy()

    if remaining_time() > 30:
        hf = slsqp_polish(best_h, best_n, 200.0, 60)
        cf = compute_c5(hf)
        if cf < best_c5:
            best_c5 = cf
            best_h = hf.copy()

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_n = len(best_h)
    final_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)

    try:
        ev = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if isinstance(ev, (float, int, np.floating)):
            ev = float(ev)
            if math.isfinite(ev) and ev < final_c5:
                final_c5 = ev
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (best_h, float(final_c5), int(best_n))
```
